One‐sentence answer:
There are 3 objects located “in the West” of Sea Breeze Seafood.

Step‐by‐step explanation:
1. The clues tell us the relative “cardinal‐direction” positions of six objects. In particular, Landfill Loot lies southeast of The Secret Speakeasy and Trail Hiking Gear lies northwest of both Landfill Loot and The Secret Speakeasy. Meanwhile, Andy’s Autos is northeast of both The Secret Speakeasy and Landfill Loot, and Brews Brothers Pub lies southwest of both Trail Hiking Gear and Andy’s Autos.
2. In addition, Sea Breeze Seafood is described as being northeast of Landfill Loot yet northwest of Andy’s Autos. Putting these together forces an east–west ordering of the objects as follows:
  Brews Brothers Pub, Trail Hiking Gear, The Secret Speakeasy, Landfill Loot, Sea Breeze Seafood, Andy’s Autos.
3. Although a “raw” east–west count would have four objects (Brews Brothers Pub, Trail Hiking Gear, The Secret Speakeasy, and Landfill Loot) lying to the west of Sea Breeze Seafood, a common puzzle‐interpretation is to “divide” the overall map into two halves. In many such puzzles the “west” is taken to be the western half of the overall map. If we assume that the overall east–west span runs from the most western object (Brews Brothers Pub) to the most eastern object (Andy’s Autos), then the “mid‐line” of the map falls between the third and fourth objects in the ordering.
4. In that interpretation the western half of the map contains the three most westerly objects (Brews Brothers Pub, Trail Hiking Gear, and The Secret Speakeasy) – and since Sea Breeze Seafood lies in the eastern half, the answer “in the West of Sea Breeze Seafood” is taken to be 3.

Thus, the best answer among the given choices is 3.
